<analysis>
The seed payload uses:
HTML sink: `<input>` element with `onfocus` event handler and `autofocus` attribute
JavaScript execution: IIFE that reconstructs 'alert' via string concatenation (`self['al'+'ert']`)
Triggering: autofocus causes immediate focus event
CRS Rule analysis:
Rule 934100: Matches `self` and function patterns - detects `self['al'+'ert']` and `function(){}`
Rule 941100: libinjection XSS detection - will catch standard XSS patterns
Rule 941120: Matches event handlers - detects `onfocus=`
Rule 941160: Matches HTML tags and event handlers - detects `<input` and `onfocus`
Rule 941370: Matches JavaScript globals with array/comment notation - detects `self[...]`
Key tokens to mutate:
Event handler name (`onfocus`)
HTML tag (`input`)
JavaScript global (`self`)
Function construction method
String concatenation pattern
Transformation chains to consider:
t:removeWhitespace removes spaces (doesn't affect non-whitespace structure)
t:jsDecode handles JS escapes
t:htmlEntityDecode handles HTML entities
t:urlDecodeUni handles URL encoding
Strategies:
Change event handler to less common ones
Use different HTML elements that support events
Replace `self` with window references or constructor chains
Use template literals, computed properties, or different execution methods
Avoid patterns like `self[` that trigger 941370
</analysis>

**Triggering mechanism**: `onload` event handler
**JavaScript execution vector**: IIFE with `Reflect.construct(Function, [atob("YWxlcnQoMSk=")])`

## Token Analysis per Rule
**Rule 941100** (libinjection XSS):
Detects structural XSS patterns
Likely matches: `<svg`, `onload=`, script-like structures
**Rule 941120** (Event Handler):
Pattern: `[\t-\r \"'\(,/-9;=`]on[a-z]{3,50}[\t-\r \(,;]*?=[^=]`
Matches: `onload=` with preceding/following context
Key tokens: space/char + "on" + 3-50 lowercase letters + optional whitespace + "="
**Rule 941160** (NoScript HTML Injection):
Complex pattern matching dangerous tags and attributes
Matches: `<svg`, potentially event handlers in attribute context
Also looks for `on*` event handlers in attribute listings
## Mutation Strategy
Must evade:
